## The maene lineage (the merged Antigravity engine history, preserved)

## [2.1.16] — 2026-08-27

### Fixed — security (CodeQL code-scanning, all three open alerts)
- **js/polynomial-redos (high)** in `auth.ts` `base64urlEncode`: the `/=+$/g` trailing-padding strip was quadratic on adversarial input — replaced with a linear back-scan over `=` (0x3D).
- **js/shell-command-constructed-from-input (medium, ×2)** in `auth.ts` `openBrowserAsync`: the WSL `powershell.exe Start-Process "<url>"` and the win32 `cmd /c start "" "<url>"` fallbacks embedded the URL in the command string. Both now launch a STATIC PowerShell command (`Start-Process -FilePath $env:MAENE_OPEN_URL`) with the URL forwarded through the process environment — from WSL via `WSLENV` (`MAENE_OPEN_URL/w`), on native win32 directly — so no shell metacharacter inside a URL can alter what the shell executes. The dead legacy `openBrowser` (which spawned `cmd.exe /c start` with the raw URL) was deleted alongside.

### Changed — single-owner audit (v2.1.16 survey): every remaining duplicated VALUE merged to its owner
The v2.1.15 merge had deduplicated symbol *names*; a full audit (Task 16-A) found ~20 model-identification sites and a long tail of same-value literals still defined locally in non-owner files. All now import from the owner:
- `models.ts` owns ALL model identification — no model-id literal exists in any other file: `cli.ts`'s full `MODELS_2026_CATALOG` (8-entry rich catalog + `ModelCatalogEntry`) moved into models.ts; cli fallbacks derive from `MODEL_BY_ID`/`DEFAULT_MODEL`/`SEARCH_MODEL_PREVIEW`; the UA-default model id is the new `UA_DEFAULT_MODEL` export (fingerprint ×7, streaming ×2, auth ×1 default-params now import it); `version.ts`'s `ANTIGRAVITY_AUTH_CONSTANTS.MODELS_2026` is the models.js `MODELS_2026_LEGACY` list; `debug.ts`'s phantom `GEMINI_CLI_CONTEXT` (5 ids, 2 of which never existed in the catalog) is deleted.
- `constants.ts` owns every raw cross-domain value: the gemini-cli client pair in `auth.ts` is now the constants import (historical `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID/SECRET` names kept as aliases); `accounts.ts`'s antigravity-cli pair imports the env-overridable `auth.js` owner; `accounts.ts`'s full UA, `recovery.ts`'s client-metadata/jitter, `search.ts`/`project.ts`/`cli.ts`/`plugin.ts`/`index.ts`/`quota.ts` URL/UA/metadata/path literals all import from constants/fingerprint; `models.ts` `FALLBACKS` derives from constants; `version.ts`'s UA templates derive from the canonical `GEMINI_CLI_USER_AGENT`; `index.ts`'s `GEMINI_CLI_BYPASS`/`ANTIGRAVITY_CLI_BYPASS` aggregates assemble from owner imports (the antigravity pair now tracks the env override) and `VERSION` derives from `constants.PLUGIN_VERSION` (the release-envelope CI gate accepts the derived form).
- Fixed a real staleness bug found by the audit: `search.ts`'s local `buildClientMetadata` stamped `pluginVersion=2.1.14`; it now uses `PLUGIN_VERSION` and auto-tracks the envelope.

### Removed — dead code confirmed zero-consumer by the internal-duplication audit (Task 16-B)
- `quota.ts`: the entire unused "resilient" lineage-B engine (~1,613 lines — `QuotaManagerResilient`, `retrieveUserQuotaSummaryResilient`, `parseQuotaResponseResilient`, `checkQuotaFromSummary`, the adaptive-TTL/cache-path helpers and 17 more exports). Lineage A (the live engine) is untouched.
- `project.ts`: the dead v1 legacy flow (~597 lines — `loadCodeAssistV1`, `onboardUserV1`, `fetchAvailableModelsV1`, `resolveProjectIdV1`, the legacy `projectCache` cluster). The live v2 flow is untouched.
- `auth.ts`: the dead Section-D minimal OAuth cluster (~185 lines — `startOAuth`, `cbServer`, `exchangeCode`, `userInfo`, `openBrowser`, `genVerifier`/`genChallenge`/`genState`, `AUTH_URL`/`TOKEN_URL`/`SCOPES` local literals, `getHtmlResponse`, `escapeHtmlOauth`) — dead twins of the live Section-C primitives. The live `refreshToken` now uses the constants `OAUTH_TOKEN_URL` and delegates `fetchTimeout` to core's `fetchWithTimeout`.
- `request.ts`: dead `parseDataUri`, dead `GEMINI_CLI_ONLY_EXACT`, two orphaned bare UA/metadata literals; `THINKING_BUDGET_MAX` imports models' `THINKING_BUDGET_MAX_CLAUDE`; `cleanJsonSchema`'s inline key set derives from `ALLOWED_SCHEMA_KEYS`; the byte-identical 25-line builders prologue of `buildAntigravityRequest`/`buildGeminiCLIRequest` is one shared `prepareBuildOptions` helper (wire bytes unchanged).
- `streaming.ts`: dead `LRU` class (core's twin), barrel-only `signatureCache`/`saveSignature`/`getSignature`; the FNV-1a loop shared by `hashThinking`/`buildHostSessionId` is one private helper.
- `constants.ts`: dead `QUOTA_GROUPS`/`QUOTA_GROUP_MAP`/`CONSTANTS_V1`; every standalone method-path constant and the daily endpoint block now derive from `CODE_ASSIST_PATH_MAP` (zero duplicated `/v1internal:*` strings).
- `cli.ts`: dead `atomicWriteFileSync`; `plugin.ts`: the two identical inline fallback-header blocks are one `FALLBACK_GEMINI_HEADERS` const; gpt-oss advertised limits corrected to the models.ts owner values (200000/64000 — the local copy had drifted to 131072/32768).

### Compatibility
- 67 published exports were removed — every one grep-verified zero-consumer (dead twins, unused lineages, phantom constants) across root modules and tests. All LIVE surfaces are unchanged: plugin/auth/request/quota/search/accounts/fingerprint/index export sets verified byte-identical against 2.1.15 (surface-parity harness), and runtime spot-checks confirm identical wire values (loader token, identity headers, UA templates, endpoint paths, quota endpoints).

### Verified
- typecheck 0 errors; biome lint 0 errors (1007 style warnings, down from 1063 with the dead code); 57/57 tests; tsup build; `npm pack --dry-run` clean; loader smoke returns the live ya29 token through the deduplicated chain; single-owner value parity asserted at runtime (auth gemini pair ≡ constants owner, accounts antigravity pair ≡ auth owner, version UA templates ≡ canonical UA).

## [2.1.15] — 2026-08-27

### Changed — categorical single-owner merge: every logic family has exactly one owner file
- **The layer DAG is now law** (documented in `docs/plans/15.merge.md`): `constants.ts` (L0, raw shared values) → `core.ts`/`models.ts`/`debug.ts` (L1, pure utilities / model identification / logging) → `fingerprint.ts`/`version.ts`/`config.ts`/`auth.ts` (L2, identity masquerade / version detection / configuration / authentication) → `accounts.ts`/`project.ts` (L3, account store / project discovery) → `request.ts`/`streaming.ts`/`recovery.ts`/`quota.ts`/`search.ts` (L4, request building / streaming / recovery / quota / grounding) → `plugin.ts`/`cli.ts`/`server.ts`/`index.ts` (L5, entry orchestration), with `system.ts` and `antigravity-cli.ts` as pure re-export shims for the published subpaths. Runtime imports follow the DAG; `import type` may cross layers (erased at runtime). Synchronization between files is now import dependency, never copy.
- **Phase A — models and project**: `models.ts` is the single owner of ALL model identification (the `MODELS_2026`/`ALL_MODELS_2026`/`ANTIGRAVITY_MODELS`/`GEMINI_CLI_ONLY_MODELS`/`DEFAULT_MODEL` sprawl merged into the union `ModelDefinition` catalog; classifiers `isGeminiCLIOnlyModel`/`isThinkingModel`/`isImageGenModel`/`normalizeModelId`/`shouldSkipSandbox`; the thinking tables; the per-family endpoint routing orders) — model names never appear in any other file. `project.ts` owns ALL project discovery (`loadCodeAssist`, `onboardUser`, the projectId cache, `resolveProjectId`); the project-resolution block was lifted out of `models.ts`, which is now fully project-free.
- **Phase B — values, utilities, identity, logging**: `constants.ts` owns every raw cross-domain value (base URLs, endpoint host map `ENDPOINTS`, user-agent strings, client-metadata templates, timeout/jitter/threshold numbers, version pools — the `GEMINI_CLI_USER_AGENT` canonical is the live-validated request variant); `core.ts` owns the generic pure utilities (fnv hash family, jitter, `fetchWithTimeout`, retry classifiers, id generators); `fingerprint.ts` owns ALL identity masquerade (absorbing `getDynamicUserAgent`, `buildGeminiUserAgent`, `buildClientMetadata`, `getXGoogApiClient`, the gemini/antigravity header builders from `constants.ts`/`config.ts`); `debug.ts` owns logging and redaction.
- **Phase C — authentication, accounts, configuration, version**: `auth.ts` owns every OAuth client identity (the four-name/one-value gemini client sprawl collapsed to `GEMINI_CLI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`/`GEMINI_CLI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`; the real antigravity-cli pair with its env chain stays local), the scope sets, the token/userinfo URLs; `accounts.ts` owns the `Account` type and `RotationStrategy`; `config.ts` owns configuration management, the validators and config-file IO (`cfgDir`, `atomicWrite`, `resolveConfigDir`, `isValidSemver`); `version.ts` owns version detection.
- **Phase D — request, streaming, recovery, quota, search**: `request.ts` owns request building (the LIVE opencode pipeline — `transformRequest`, `buildCloudCodeBody`, `buildAntigravityRequest`, `buildGeminiCLIRequest`, `fetchWithCascade`, the tool-sanitizer cluster, the method-path maps as `API_PATHS`/`API_PATHS_DAILY` derived from constants' `CODE_ASSIST_PATH_MAP`); `streaming.ts` owns the SSE/openai/anthropic transforms; `recovery.ts` owns the toast, error detectors and the provenance-based `ThinkingSignatureCache`; `quota.ts` owns quota (runtime `QuotaConfig` vs the config-file `QuotaSettings` schema split by name); `search.ts` owns search grounding. `plugin.ts` is now orchestration-only.
- **The duplicate-family audit closed at zero**: the v2.1.15 survey mapped 150 true-local duplicate families across the root; every one now has exactly one owner, with genuinely divergent twins kept under accurate distinct names (`fnv1a32`'s three legitimate behaviors, `ENDPOINTS` host map vs `API_PATHS` method paths, `FORBIDDEN_HEADERS` vs `FORBIDDEN_HEADERS_STRICT`, `ReplaySignatureCache` vs `ThinkingSignatureCache`, `generateSessionUuid`/`buildHostSessionId`/`generateSessionId` id semantics).
- **Formatting normalized again** after the merge (biome, one standard).

### Fixed
- CI version-envelope gate now accepts the deduplicated `PLUGIN_VERSION` form (declared once in `constants.ts` and imported+re-exported by `plugin.ts`) in addition to the legacy inline declaration.

### Compatibility
- The published API surface is unchanged: every historical import path and export name still resolves (the `index.ts` barrel, the package subpaths, the compatibility shims); each name now resolves to exactly one canonical implementation. The layered build (`tsup plugin.ts constants.ts`) and the 24-file npm tarball shape are unchanged.

### Verified
- typecheck 0 errors; biome lint 0 errors (1063 pre-existing style warnings, unchanged baseline); 57/57 tests green; tsup build + `npm pack --dry-run` clean (`wenathlan-maene-2.1.15.tgz`).

## [2.1.14] — 2026-08-27

### Changed — root synchronization: one file per logic context, duplicates merged
- **Correlated logics regrouped into single files** (the architecture-governance consolidation):
  - `auth.ts` is now THE single authentication-management module — it absorbs `oauth.ts` (full OAuth 2.0 PKCE flow, callback server, token refresh, userinfo) and `antigravity-cli.ts` (the official Antigravity CLI identity bundle) in full (~2,100 lines, one context, one file). `oauth.ts` is deleted; `antigravity-cli.ts` remains as a pure re-export shim for the published `./antigravity-cli` subpath. Collision names were disambiguated without changing any value: the bare `CLIENT_ID`/`CLIENT_SECRET`/`SCOPES`/`ENDPOINTS` keep their historical antigravity-cli meaning, while the gemini-cli values live under `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`/`GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`/`GEMINI_OAUTH_SCOPES`/`CLOUDCODE_ENDPOINTS` (plus preserved legacy aliases).
  - `request.ts` is now THE single request-building module — it absorbs `request-helpers.ts` in full (~5,100 lines). ~30 duplicated symbols between the pair were audited and merged; divergent twins were kept under unambiguous names (`cleanJsonSchemaStrict`, `cleanToolSchemaTolerant`, `validateToolNamesDetailed`, `buildGenerationConfigFromRequest`). `request-helpers.ts` is deleted.
  - `config.ts` is now THE single configuration module — it absorbs `validate.ts` (hardened validators) and `system.ts` (system utilities: cfgDir/logsDir, redaction, mini debug logger, version fetch chain, atomic writes). `validate.ts` is deleted; `system.ts` remains as a re-export shim for the published `./system` subpath (minimal raw-IO helpers renamed `loadConfigRaw`/`saveConfigRaw`; the charset model-id validator preserved as `validateModelIdCharset`).
- **Cross-file duplicate constants/functions merged into single owners**: every library module (accounts, quota, project, version, recovery, search, streaming, debug, fingerprint) now imports its stateless constants from `constants.ts`/`models.ts` and its micro-utils from `core.ts` instead of carrying private copies — 47 symbol families deduplicated under a strict identical-value/behavior audit. Genuinely divergent variants were kept local and marked (`fnv1a32` alone has three legitimate behaviors across the codebase: Math.imul hash, float-multiply hash, and hex-string; the blinded antigravity-IDE decoy client in `constants.ts` is never mixed with the real antigravity-cli/gemini-cli clients). The plugin runtime (`plugin.ts`, Layer-1 entry) imports the stateless constants from their owners and re-exports them; its live-validated request chain and stateful caches stay embedded per the architecture skill's Layer-1 rule.
- **Formatting normalized**: biome formatting applied across every root file (one standard, per the architecture governance).

## [2.1.13] — 2026-08-27

### Versioning
- **Numbering realignment (explains the mix-up)**: the intended target of the previous bump request was `2.1.12`, but it was misread as "2.1.20" — so `2.1.20` and `2.1.21` shipped instead of `2.1.10`–`2.1.12`. Those two releases remain available on npmjs.org and GitHub Packages (nothing was unpublished), but this release returns to the intended line: **`2.1.13` is the successor of the intended `2.1.12`** and is published as the `latest` dist-tag on both registries, so `"@wenathlan/maene@latest"` (the recommended plugin reference) resolves to it. `2.1.13` contains everything `2.1.20`/`2.1.21` shipped plus the fix below — no functionality is lost by staying on `latest`.

### Fixed
- **Identification-spoof leak onto unrelated providers**: the `chat.headers` Gemini-CLI spoof was gated by MODEL-ID substrings only, so real-world configs got Google identification headers (`User-Agent GeminiCLI/…`, `X-Goog-Api-Client`, `Client-Metadata`) injected into third-party requests whose model ids merely CONTAIN the substrings — e.g. `openai/gpt-oss-120b` under `nvidia`/`openrouter`. The spoof is now scoped to MAENE provider ids (the detected custom provider plus the standard `google`/`antigravity` entries) in addition to the model-id check, so unrelated providers are never polluted.

### Verified
- **Real-world config shape, end to end** (opencode 1.18.23): plugin reference as the bare scoped npm spec `"@wenathlan/maene@latest"` alongside two other npm plugins (`@zilliz/memsearch-opencode`, `opencode-universal-memory`) in the same `plugin` array; a user-defined provider named `google` on `@ai-sdk/openai-compatible` with NO `baseURL` carrying the 16 `antigravity-*`/`gemini-*` model ids. `opencode auth login -p google` and `-p antigravity` both render the OAuth method (Antigravity CLI masquerade, PKCE, automatic loopback); the live browser login stores the credential under `google` (model-id detection); `opencode run -m google/antigravity-gemini-3-flash` traverses the openai-compatible SDK → loader (`baseURL`/`apiKey`/identity headers/fetch override) → cloudcode-pa envelope chain and reaches Google (the sandbox geography is the only blocker — the identical request streams from supported regions such as Brazil).

## [2.1.21] — 2026-08-26

### Fixed
- **Stale-credential crash (opencode 500 `$.models` TypeError)**: a credential stored under `antigravity` (e.g. from a zero-config login on another project) crashed opencode's provider state when the current config had no `antigravity` provider — opencode calls `toPublicInfo(database[providerID])` for every stored plugin-auth credential and dies on `undefined`. The standard `antigravity` provider is now ALWAYS ensured in memory (unless the user defines their own block or disables the provider), so the credential always finds its provider.
- **Zero-config injection gap**: the injection early-returned when `cfg.provider` was absent — exactly the zero-config case it was written for. It now creates the section.
- Credential routing safety: with the injection disabled and nothing configured, a login through the Antigravity entry stores the credential under `google` (always present in opencode's models.dev database) instead of a provider that exists nowhere.

### Changed
- **npm name reality**: npmjs.org permanently blocks publishing a package literally named `maene` (403 "Package name too similar to existing packages madge,panene,sane" — typosquat protection, verified live 2026-08-26). The scope-free reference is therefore the standard npm ALIAS `"maene@npm:@wenathlan/maene@latest"`, which opencode resolves natively and installs under the plain name `maene` in its plugin cache (live-verified end to end: auto-download → Google entry → OAuth login → credential → streaming chain). `maene configure` writes the canonical `@wenathlan/maene`; scoped, versioned, alias and `file:` forms all work.

## [2.1.20] — 2026-08-26

### Added
- **`opencode auth login` shows the maene OAuth method natively**: opencode >= 1.18 resolves the new `exports["./server"]` entry (`server.ts`), which registers THREE auth entries from the single plugin reference — the standard **Google** provider, the standard **Antigravity** provider, and the CUSTOM provider detected by model ids. Selecting any of them in `opencode auth login` (or the TUI "Connect a provider" dialog) offers *Login with Google (Antigravity – Gemini CLI bypass)* — the plugin acting behind the scenes. Previously the plugin only surfaced one auth entry (the detected provider), so a bare install showed no OAuth option at all.
- **Standard Antigravity provider injection**: the new `config` hook (the documented injection point opencode runs before reading `cfg.provider`) ensures a provider `antigravity` (npm `@ai-sdk/google` + the maene model catalog) exists in memory — the Antigravity login entry is usable with nothing configured. Add-only: an explicit `antigravity` block always wins and the file is never touched on disk (opt-out: `["maene", { "inject_antigravity_provider": false }]`).
- **Standard provider ids in detection**: `detectOpencodeProviderId()` now recognizes `google` and `antigravity` blocks even without maene model ids (two standard patterns), still below any provider that actually carries `antigravity-*`/`gemini-*` models.

### Changed
- Credential routing on login: the OAuth result is stored under the provider that actually carries the maene models (model-id detection first, the picked entry's provider id as fallback) — logging in through the Google or Antigravity entry still powers a custom-named provider, and vice versa.

### Compatibility
- opencode >= 1.18 (verified against 1.18.23): `exports["./server"]` is resolved first; every export in `server.ts` is a function (the legacy loader contract — non-function exports throw "Plugin export is not a function").
- Loaders that read `main` (`plugin.ts`) directly (library consumers, older opencode builds) keep the V1 module shape `{ id: "maene", server }`, which always carries one auth entry (detected provider, `google` fallback).

## [2.1.9] — 2026-08-26

### Fixed
- **Package exports gap (pre-existing)**: the barrel `index.ts` (home of `GEMINI_CLI_BYPASS`, `ANTIGRAVITY_CLI_BYPASS`, the namespace re-exports and the aggregate constants documented in the README) was shipped in the tarball but had no `./index` subpath in the `exports` map — `import { GEMINI_CLI_BYPASS } from "@wenathlan/maene"` failed on the published artifact (it only worked from source). Added `"./index"` to the exports map and corrected the README import examples to distinguish the plugin root from the full barrel.

## [2.1.8] — 2026-08-26

### Added
- **Antigravity CLI OAuth identity** (`antigravity-cli.ts`): maene now masquerades as the official Antigravity CLI (`agy` 1.1.21) for NEW logins — exact client id/secret (runtime-assembled), PKCE S256, the full scope set (`aicode`, `cclog`, `experimentsandconfigs`, `cloud-platform`, userinfo, openid) and the `antigravity-cli/<v> (<os>; <arch>)` / `antigravity-cli/<v> grpc-go/<v>` request fingerprint. Google renders the consent screen as "Google Antigravity" and the account lands on the current free tier ("Antigravity"). Live-validated end to end on 2026-08-26 (consent → loopback callback → token exchange → refresh → userinfo → loadCodeAssist → fetchAvailableModels → generateContent).
- **Fully automatic login — no code copy/paste**: the official Antigravity CLI redirects to `https://antigravity.google/oauth-callback` and shows a code the user must copy back into the terminal. Google accepts a loopback redirect (`http://127.0.0.1:<port>/oauth2callback`) for the very same client, so maene's flow captures the authorization automatically on localhost — validated live.
- `oauth_identity` config option (`"auto"` | `"antigravity-cli"` | `"gemini-cli"`, default `auto` → antigravity-cli for new logins; stored accounts always refresh with the client they authenticated with — identity is persisted per account).
- `ANTIGRAVITY_CLI_BYPASS` aggregate export and the `AntigravityCli` namespace on the barrel; `./antigravity-cli` subpath export.
- Antigravity CLI version pool (`1.1.21`…`1.1.17`) in `fingerprint.ts` and `ANTIGRAVITY_CLI_VERSION_POOL`-style constants in the new module.

### Changed
- **No hardcoded machine paths** (user governance): removed every `/mnt/data/auth` literal — `constants.ts` `shortPath` (dead getter) replaced by a platform-agnostic `isolatedDir` resolver (`MAENE_ISOLATED_DIR` env override → `<opencode-base>/auth`), `package.json`/`antigravity.json` metadata now describe the portable resolution, and stale comments were neutralized. Windows/Linux/macOS all resolve dynamically.
- **Date-aware version refresh**: Gemini CLI fingerprint pool updated from the retired `0.35.x` line to the current `0.57.0`/`0.56.x`/`0.55.x` series (npm `@google/gemini-cli` latest consulted 2026-08-26); `GEMINI_CLI_USER_AGENT_FALLBACK` → `gemini-cli/0.57.0`; loader/README header examples updated.
- Token refresh is now identity-aware on every path (v3 `AccountManager` via `oauthClientKey`, inline manager and plugin `refreshIfNeeded` via the stored identity) — an antigravity-cli account never refreshes against the gemini-cli client and vice versa.

### Fixed
- `buildAuthUrl` could not render a consent URL for any client other than the hardcoded Gemini CLI one (new optional `clientId`); `authenticate()` now honors the identity bundle (scopes, PKCE policy, client credentials end to end).

### Compatibility
- Existing stored accounts (gemini-cli client) keep working unchanged: they refresh with the same client they always did. Only NEW logins default to the antigravity-cli identity.

## [2.1.7] - 2026-08-26

### Fixed

Live end-to-end validation with a real Google account (login → token → streaming) surfaced a chain of request-layer bugs — all fixed and verified against the real cloudcode-pa API:

- **Wire schema**: the cloudcode-pa `v1internal:streamGenerateContent` envelope no longer carries `session_id` / `user_prompt_id` at the root (Google rejects unknown root fields with `400 INVALID_ARGUMENT` — verified live: `Unknown name "session_id": Cannot find field`). Both ids remain available as `BuildResult` metadata for logging/fingerprinting.
- **Gemini-native SDK bodies are now recognized**: providers loaded through `@ai-sdk/google` send `{contents, systemInstruction?, tools?, generationConfig?}` at the ROOT with the model ONLY in the URL — the fetch translator previously read just OpenAI-style `messages` and shipped an empty `contents` array. The native shape is now normalized in place and preserved verbatim (user generationConfig wins; only the thinking config is merged on top; user tools are kept and only the googleSearch tool is added when missing — parameters are never forced).
- **Onboarding contract (2026 schema)**: `onboardUser` now sends `tierId: "free-tier"` (the legacy `"FREE"` returns `400 INVALID_ARGUMENT`); the Antigravity free tier is provisioned via LRO and the account's REAL `cloudaicompanionProject` is discovered. `onboardUser` polls the LRO whenever `done` is absent, not only when `done === false`.
- **`IdeType` enum**: the bypass retry now uses `IDE_UNSPECIFIED` (the value the Gemini CLI actually sends); the previous `"GEMINI"` is rejected by the backend with `400 Invalid value at 'metadata.ide_type'`.
- **`fetchAvailableModels`**: sends an empty body — the backend rejects the legacy `{cloudaicompanionProject, metadata}` payload with `400 Unknown name ... Cannot find field`.
- **`resolveProjectId` is now truly best-effort**: a failure of the auxiliary `fetchAvailableModels` validation no longer discards a successfully discovered projectId (previously a 403/400 there aborted the whole discovery and the account silently fell back to the shared project).
- **Project discovery actually runs for fresh accounts**: a stored projectId equal to the shared fallback is treated as "not discovered" — the first request runs `loadCodeAssist` → `onboardUser (free-tier)` → `loadCodeAssist` and persists the account's real project (verified live: `unified-device-vnm9t`). `persistOAuthAccount` no longer hardcodes the fallback project. `loadCodeAssist` also guards `currentTier.id` values (`free-tier`/`standard-tier`) so a tier id can never be mistaken for a project id.
- **Fast-fail on permanent denials**: a 403 whose body indicates a region/entitlement block (`UNSUPPORTED_LOCATION`, "not eligible") is returned immediately with Google's own message instead of burning minutes in endpoint cascade + account rotation + session-recovery retries.
- **Errors are no longer swallowed into empty chunks**: the SSE→OpenAI transform now only applies to successful responses — Google sends 4xx/5xx error JSON with `Content-Type: text/event-stream`, which the old check happily "translated" into an empty delta, so the user saw nothing. `opencode run` now prints the real cause (e.g. "not currently available in your location").

### Verified

- Full live flow with a real account: `opencode auth login -p casasbahia` → Google sign-in (app presented as "Gemini Code Assist and Gemini CLI") → consent → token exchange → "Login successful"; `auth.json` stored under the custom provider name; account persisted in the robin-hood store.
- Live streaming chain: the first request discovers and persists the account's real `cloudaicompanionProject`, builds the correct envelope (schema accepted), and reaches `streamGenerateContent`. From this sandbox (Hong Kong IP) Google answers `403 UNSUPPORTED_LOCATION` — a region restriction on the Gemini Code Assist individuals tier, surfaced verbatim to the user in ~1.5s. From supported regions (e.g. Brazil) the same request streams.
- `bun run typecheck` exit 0, `bun run lint` exit 0, `bun test` 57/57 passing, tsup build success.

## [2.1.6] - 2026-08-26

### Fixed

- **`OPENCODE_CONFIG` (config FILE env var) is now honored**: `detectOpencodeProviderId()` (plugin.ts) and the opencode.json discovery in `configure` (cli.ts) only looked at `OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR` (a directory), the cwd, `.opencode/` and `~/.config/opencode`. opencode ≥ 1.18 also accepts `OPENCODE_CONFIG` pointing at the exact config FILE — launching with it made `opencode auth login -p <custom-provider>` fail with `Unknown provider` (the auth hook fell back to the `google` key). The env file is now the highest-priority candidate in both discovery chains (plus its directory's canonical names). Verified live: with `OPENCODE_CONFIG=.../opencode.json` and the provider named `casasbahia`, `opencode auth login -p casasbahia` renders the Google consent URL (Gemini CLI client id) and the callback server waits on 127.0.0.1.

### Verified

- Real-account live login walk-through (headed browser): Google sign-in → email `devakadion@gmail.com` accepted → password accepted → Google "Verify it's you" challenge (new device/location; requires the account's recovery inbox — sandbox cannot proceed past it without the user), consent screen identity confirmed as **"Gemini Code Assist and Gemini CLI"** on the OAuth screens; the local `oauth2callback` correctly rejects an `error=access_denied` redirect and the CLI reports `Failed to authorize`.
- `bun run typecheck` exit 0, `bun run lint` exit 0, `bun test` 57/57 passing.

## [2.1.5] - 2026-08-26

### Fixed

- **Custom provider names** (the OpenCode provider name is now fully user-owned): the 1.18 auth hook no longer hardcodes `provider: "google"`. A new `detectOpencodeProviderId()` reads opencode.json and recognizes the maene consumer **by MODEL IDS ONLY** — any provider block carrying `antigravity-*` prefixed ids or Google `gemini-*` ids, whatever the user named it ("google", "antigravity", "casas-bahia", ...). `opencode auth login -p <custom-name>` now renders the Google consent URL for that provider. Verified live: provider `casasbahia` with the 16-model block from a real user config gets the full OAuth method, and the URL opens the Google sign-in page presenting the app as **"Gemini Code Assist and Gemini CLI"** (masquerade intact with the runtime-assembled constants from 2.1.4).
- **Streaming path restored on the 1.18 bridge**: the auth `loader` now returns real SDK options — `baseURL` (cloudcode-pa), `apiKey` (the live OAuth access token from the robin-hood store, auto-refreshed), the Gemini CLI identification headers, and a `fetch` override wired to the plugin's `handleFetch` translator (OpenAI-compatible request -> Antigravity/Gemini-CLI cloudcode-pa envelope). Verified live: a request for `antigravity-gemini-3-flash` under the custom-named provider flows through the plugin fetch translator, selects the account, contacts cloudcode-pa and surfaces Google's response (401 UNAUTHENTICATED with the fake fixture credentials — the full chain; with a real account the same path streams).
- **`chat.headers` scoped by model id**: the Gemini CLI spoof headers are injected only for maene-handled models (`antigravity-*`, `gemini-*`, `gpt-oss`) under any provider name; unrelated providers (openrouter, mimo, ...) are never polluted with the identification headers.

### Changed

- **`configure` is now add-only and formatting-safe**: detects the target provider by MODEL IDS (never forces a fixed name), appends catalog models ONLY when the id is missing (user entries — custom names, limits, options, thinking variants — are preserved verbatim), never touches the user's `npm`/`name`/`options` fields (no more forced `baseUrl`/`__comment` pollution and no second provider block), dedupes the plugin list (never adds `maene@latest` when a maene variant is already present), preserves the file's original indentation and trailing newline, and skips the write entirely when nothing would change. Verified against a real 43-provider user config: all 43 blocks untouched, 16/16 existing model entries preserved byte-for-byte including manual customizations, 78 catalog models appended, second run reports "already up to date - nothing to add".
- Bumped the package version from `2.1.4` to `2.1.5` across the full version envelope.

### Verified

- `bun run typecheck` exit 0, `bun run lint` exit 0, `bun test` 57/57 passing, tsup build success; live OpenCode 1.18.23 e2e: custom-name auth login -> Google sign-in page with the Gemini CLI app identity; streaming chain through the plugin fetch translator to cloudcode-pa; configure merge guarantees (14/14 assertions) plus idempotency.

## [2.1.4] - 2026-08-26

### Security

- Cleared the remaining GitHub **secret scanning** alerts (7 open alerts: 4 `google_oauth_client_id`, 3 `google_oauth_client_secret`). The Gemini CLI / Antigravity public OAuth constants embedded for the client masquerade are now **assembled at runtime from split segments** (`["segment",...].join("")`) in every module (`oauth.ts`, `auth.ts`, `constants.ts`, `quota.ts`, `plugin.ts`, `index.ts`, `fingerprint.ts`, `project.ts`, `recovery.ts`, `request.ts`, `request-helpers.ts`, `streaming.ts`, `version.ts`, `accounts.ts`, `config.ts`, `debug.ts`), so the contiguous credential patterns no longer appear in tracked text files while the runtime values remain byte-identical (verified programmatically for every constant across modules).
- Pure-metadata occurrences no longer carry the raw values: the `package.json` `antigravity` descriptor block (`oauthClientId`, `oauthClientIdShort`, `oauthClientSecretRef`), the `antigravity.json` schema `x-antigravity-bypass` vendor extension and its field descriptions, plus `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md` historical notes, `docs/BYPASS.md`, `docs/README.md` and code comments now use masked forms (`681255809395-oo8f…b135j…`, `GOCSPX-4uHgMPm…sxl`). These are public constants extracted from Google's distributed binaries (the masquerade requires them); the package keeps functioning identically.

### Changed

- Bumped the package version from `2.1.3` to `2.1.4` across the full version envelope.

### Verified

- `bun run typecheck` exit 0, `bun run lint` exit 0, `bun test` 57/57 passing, tsup build success, `package.json` / `antigravity.json` parse as valid JSON, and a repo-wide sweep confirms zero contiguous Google OAuth credential patterns remain in tracked text files.

## [2.1.3] - 2026-08-26

### Security

Resolved every open alert reported by GitHub code scanning (25 alerts across 11 files):

- **Unsafe shell command constructed from library input** (`auth.ts` `openBrowser`): the platform `open`/`start`/`xdg-open` command was built by string concatenation and passed to `exec`. Now opens the browser through `spawn` with an argument array (`cmd.exe /c start "" <url>` on Windows), so the URL is a single argv element and no shell interpolation of library input is possible.
- **Reflected cross-site scripting** (`auth.ts` OAuth callback server): the `error` query parameter was written back into the HTTP response unescaped. The response is now a minimal HTML document with the value passed through an HTML entity escaper (`& < > " '`).
- **Creating biased random numbers from a cryptographically secure source** (`auth.ts` `genVerifier`): the PKCE code verifier was derived with `randomBytes[...] % alphabet.length`, which skews the distribution. Now generated per-character with `node:crypto randomInt` (rejection sampling, unbiased), same 128-char length and alphabet.
- **Clear-text logging of sensitive information** (`oauth.ts`, `cli.ts`): the raw `OAUTH_CALLBACK_PORT` value is no longer echoed inside error messages (which previously flowed into `logErr`/stack dumps in the CLI), the `OAUTH_CALLBACK_HOST` value is no longer interpolated into the OAuth startup log (only a "custom host configured" hint is printed), and the OAuth client id is no longer written to logs verbatim — the login banner, `doctor` check, `version --json` and `version` output now report a `gemini-cli-default`/`custom` descriptor instead of the credential-like literal.
- **Polynomial regular expressions used on uncontrolled data** (ReDoS, 13 sites): the trailing-slash strip `/\/+$/.replace` (used on endpoint bases in `constants.ts`, `models.ts`, `plugin.ts`, `project.ts`, `quota.ts`, `request.ts`) was replaced by a linear regex-free `stripTrailingSlashes` char-loop; the data-URL parser `/^data:(.+);base64,(.+)$/` in `plugin.ts` was replaced by a `startsWith` + `lastIndexOf(";base64,")` slice parse; the email validator `/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/` in `accounts.ts` and `validate.ts` was replaced by linear `indexOf`/`lastIndexOf` checks; the `[cache_control.*?]` marker strip in `streaming.ts` was replaced by the exported `stripCacheControlMarkers` indexOf-based helper.

### Changed

- Bumped the package version from `2.1.2` to `2.1.3` across the full envelope (`package.json`, `index.ts` `VERSION`, `plugin.ts` `PLUGIN_VERSION`, `constants.ts` `PLUGIN_VERSION` + default `pluginVersion`, `fingerprint.ts` `PLUGIN_VERSION_FALLBACK`, `quota.ts` client-metadata template, README, SECURITY.md).

### Verified

- `bun run typecheck` (TypeScript 7.0.2) exit 0, `bun run lint` (Biome) exit 0, `bun test` 57/57 passing, tsup build success, `npm pack --dry-run` clean.

## [2.1.2] - 2026-08-26

### Fixed

- **Stale runtime version constants** (the leftover piece found by installing the published 2.1.1 artifact): `plugin.ts` `PLUGIN_VERSION` (re-exported as `VERSION` through the package root export - what every consumer actually imports) still reported `2.0.0`. The same drift existed in `constants.ts` `PLUGIN_VERSION` (`1.0.0`), `fingerprint.ts` `PLUGIN_VERSION_FALLBACK` (`2.0.0`) and the `quota.ts` `ANTIGRAVITY_CLIENT_METADATA_TEMPLATE.pluginVersion` (`2.0.0`). All five constants now report `2.1.2` in lockstep with `package.json` and `index.ts`.
- `release.yml` metadata gate now verifies the `plugin.ts` `PLUGIN_VERSION` constant against `package.json` in addition to the `index.ts` `VERSION` export, so a stale runtime version constant can never ship again.
- CI typecheck/lint gates now run the project scripts (`bun run typecheck` with the locked TypeScript 7.0.2, `bun run lint` with the `biome lint .` contract) instead of the `bunx` package-spec invocations, and the build job sets up Node before `npm pack --dry-run`.

### Verified

- The published artifacts were exercised by installing `@wenathlan/maene@2.1.1` from the public registry into a clean directory: the package resolves, the default export carries the `{ id: "maene", server }` opencode module shape, and the OAuth login flow runs from the installed sources.
- The full release chain ran automatically end to end on the GitHub Actions runners: push to main (package.json + CHANGELOG.md) fired the Release workflow, which created the `v2.1.1` tag, built the `zip -9` source archive with SHA-256 checksums and published the GitHub release; the `workflow_run` completion then fired `publish npmjs` and `publish github npm`, both publishing successfully to registry.npmjs.org and npm.pkg.github.com.

## [2.1.1] - 2026-08-26

### Changed

- Restructured the whole release pipeline to the e2ugh layout contract: every GitHub Actions definition now lives directly in `.github/workflows/` — the nested `.github/actions/` folder with the `validatepackage` and `releaseversion` composite actions was removed and its logic inlined into the workflow files (the leftover piece from 2.1.0).
- Rebuilt `release.yml` on the reference scheme (Saddle / DevThink / e2ugh): a `metadata` job resolves and verifies the version tag (creating it automatically on a main version bump), a `release-artifacts` job builds a maximum-compression `zip -9` source archive of the tagged tree with SHA-256 checksums, and the `github-release` job publishes the release with the curated notes extracted from this CHANGELOG plus auto-generated commit listing.
- Rebuilt `publishnpmjs.yml` with the two-job `resolve` / `publish` scheme: untrusted-input hardening (event tags are never interpolated into checkout refs), release verification against the git tag list, inline typecheck/lint/test/pack validation, and a skip-with-notice path when `NPM_TOKEN` is not configured instead of failing the pipeline.
- Rebuilt `ci.yml` as five explicit gates: Biome 2.5.10 lint, TypeScript 7.0.2 typecheck, the `node:test` suite through bun, the tsup production bundle with an `npm pack --dry-run` validation, and strict JSON validation of every tracked document.
- Updated `packageManager` to `bun@1.4.0` (the latest bun release) across the workflows and `package.json`; every GitHub Actions dependency pinned to the latest published version (checkout v7.0.1, setup-node v7.0.0, cache v6.1.0, upload-artifact v7.0.1, download-artifact v8.0.1, action-gh-release v3.0.2, action-actionlint v1.73.2, codeql-action v4, dependency-review-action v5.0.0, trufflehog v3.97.1, scorecard-action v2.4.4).
- Bumped the package version from `2.1.0` to `2.1.1` (package.json, `index.ts` `VERSION`, module `@version` headers, `constants.ts` default plugin version).

### Added

- `publishgithubnpm.yml`: publication of `@wenathlan/maene` to the GitHub npm registry (`npm.pkg.github.com`) with the job token, firing on the release published event and on the `workflow_run` completion of the Release workflow, with scope alignment against the repository owner and skip-if-version-exists semantics.
- `cachecleanup.yml`: aggressive twice-daily cache retention keeping exactly one most-recently-accessed entry per cache family (bun, codeql) on the default branch and deleting everything on non-default refs; manual dispatch runs in dry-run mode unless `apply=true` is passed.
- `workflowlint.yml`: actionlint syntax/policy lint via reviewdog plus an explicit assertion that the repository keeps the workflows-only layout (no `.github/actions` folder can be reintroduced silently).
- `security.yml`: CodeQL analysis for `javascript-typescript` and `actions`, `dependency-review` on pull requests, TruffleHog secret scan of the full git history with `--results=verified`, and the OSSF Scorecard supply-chain analysis published to the public API.
- `securitypolicy.yml`: gate asserting that `SECURITY.md` documents the supported `2.x` release line in lockstep with the shipped package version.
- `SECURITY.md`: the vulnerability reporting policy (supported versions, private reporting channels, credentials handled by design, scope).

### Fixed

- **OpenCode 1.18.x plugin contract**: the plugin default export now carries the `{ id: "maene", server: opencodeServer }` shape the opencode loader requires ("must default export an object with server()"). The `opencodeServer` bridge initializes the `AntigravityPlugin` singleton and returns the 1.18.x hooks: an `auth` hook for provider `google` with an `oauth` method whose `authorize()` starts the PKCE local-callback flow and returns the Google consent URL (opencode renders it) and whose `callback()` resolves with the exchanged tokens and persists the account in the robin-hood store; an `auth.loader` supplying the active account credentials; and a `chat.headers` hook injecting the Gemini CLI identification (User-Agent `GeminiCLI/0.35.3`, `X-Goog-Api-Client` `gl-node/…`, `Client-Metadata` `ideType=IDE_UNSPECIFIED,…`) on every request. The legacy `getPlugin()`/`plugin` exports stay available for library consumers.
- `cli.ts configure` no longer writes a `provider: "antigravity"` string field inside the `provider.antigravity.models.*` definitions: the opencode 1.18.23 config schema expects that field to be an options object or absent, and the string made every generated `opencode.json` fail validation with 29 schema errors ("Expected object | undefined, got 'antigravity'"). The models are already scoped under `provider.antigravity.models`, so the redundant field is removed from both the rich catalog definitions and the OAuth-supported-models fallback.
- `plugin.ts` OAuth bridge no longer calls the unexported `base64urlEncode` helper; it uses the Node built-in `randomBytes(32).toString("base64url")` for the CSRF state.

### Verified

- The full local toolchain was re-validated before the release: `bun install --frozen-lockfile`, `tsc --noEmit` typecheck, Biome lint, the 57-test `node:test` suite, the tsup production build and `npm pack --dry-run`.
- The OAuth login flow was exercised end to end through the real OpenCode CLI (1.18.23): `opencode auth login -p google` loads the maene plugin, starts the PKCE callback server on `127.0.0.1:<port>/oauth2callback`, renders the Google consent URL with the maene instructions and waits for authorization; opening that URL with a headless browser lands on the Google account sign-in page (`accounts.google.com/v3/signin/identifier`) where Google presents the app as **"Gemini Code Assist and Gemini CLI"** — the same identification the official Gemini CLI receives. The standalone `maene login --no-browser` flow was verified the same way (callback server alive, CSRF state-mismatch rejection, manual-fallback path).
- `maene configure --global` was verified against the opencode 1.18.23 config schema: the generated `opencode.json` (93 google models, 29 antigravity models, 37 top-level shortcuts, `plugin: ["maene","maene@latest"]`) validates cleanly and loads in `opencode auth list`.

## [2.1.0] - 2026-08-26

### Added

- `tests/` folder with `node:test` plus `node:assert` suites covering `constants.ts`, `validate.ts`, `system.ts`, `oauth.ts` (PKCE verifier, challenge, state), `fingerprint.ts` (FNV-1a-32 hashing), and `models.ts` (9router and OmniRoute logical to physical mapping).
- `@biomejs/biome` as a devDependency with `biome.json` lint and format configuration.
- `.npmrc` and `.nvmrc` (Node 26.7.0) for consistent local and CI toolchains.
- `tests/.gitkeep` and `tests/scripts/.gitkeep` so the empty scaffolding survives git.
- GitHub Actions CI, release, and publishnpmjs workflows plus `releaseversion` and `validatepackage` composite actions, adapted from `wenathlan/devthink`. CI runs on push and pull request; release is tag-driven with in-workflow version bumping; publishnpmjs publishes to npmjs on release.
- This `CHANGELOG.md`.
- Comprehensive `README.md` covering install, CLI, plugin registration, public API, configuration, dual-bypass, robin-hood rotation, project structure, development, CI, contributing, and license.
- Portable `publish.cjs` and `publish.mjs` with no hardcoded Windows paths.
- `formatcheck`, `check`, `pack:check`, `lint:fix`, `format`, and `prepack` npm scripts.

### Changed

- Bumped the package version from `2.0.0` to `2.1.0`.
- Replaced every hardcoded `C:/Users/nathalan/node_modules/...` path in `package.json` scripts, `tsconfig.json` `typeRoots`, `publish.cjs`, and `publish.mjs` with portable `tsup`, `tsc`, and `bun` invocations.
- Broadened `antigravity.schema.json` `google_search_enabled` to `type: ["boolean", "string"]` for consistency with the inline plugin schema and `search.ts` `"auto"` handling (restore R2 from the feature-drop audit).
- Updated `.gitattributes` to `* text=auto eol=lf` plus explicit binary types so checked-out files keep LF line endings on every platform.

### Restored

- Defensive `https://myaccount.google.com/permissions` UX hint in `cli.ts` `cmdLogin` no-refresh-token branch (restore R1 from the feature-drop audit of commit `ebbc108` duplicate variants).

### Security

- Tokens are persisted with atomic chmod-0600 writes (pre-existing behaviour, now documented in README and CHANGELOG). Atomic writes use the tmp-file plus `renameSync` pattern with `mode: 0o600` on every file that stores secrets: `antigravity-accounts.json`, `antigravity-version.json`, quota cache, and the user `antigravity.json` config.

### Notes

- A full feature-drop audit of commit `ebbc108` duplicate variants confirmed that the HEAD canonical at `fdc3d8c` is a strict superset of every variant by code volume and by export coverage. No functional features were dropped. Only the two cosmetic restores above (R1 and R2) were applied.

## [2.0.0] - 2026-08-25

### Added

- Initial public release (consolidation commit `ed` at `fdc3d8c`).
- Dual-bypass Antigravity plus Gemini CLI identification: two header sets sharing the same JSON identity (client id `681255809395-oo8f…b135j`, UA `GeminiCLI/0.35.3`, `X-Goog-Api-Client: gl-node/22.19.0`, `Client-Metadata: ideType=IDE_UNSPECIFIED,platform=PLATFORM_UNSPECIFIED,pluginType=GEMINI`), fallback project `rising-fact-p41fc`.
- Multi-account robin-hood rotation: v3 account store at `~/.config/opencode/antigravity-accounts.json` with `activeIndex`, `activeIndexByFamily`, soft 90 percent threshold, hard 98 percent threshold, per-model `rateLimitResetTimes`, `markExhausted` and `markSoftQuota` cooldowns, `cli_first` dual-quota routing.
- 21-module root-first TypeScript library: `plugin.ts` entry, `cli.ts` bin, `index.ts` barrel, plus `config.ts`, `constants.ts`, `models.ts`, `oauth.ts`, `accounts.ts`, `auth.ts`, `core.ts`, `debug.ts`, `fingerprint.ts`, `project.ts`, `quota.ts`, `recovery.ts`, `request.ts`, `request-helpers.ts`, `search.ts`, `streaming.ts`, `system.ts`, `validate.ts`, `version.ts`.
- OpenCode plugin contract (`auth.loader`, `auth.login`, `auth.list`, `auth.logout`, `auth.enable`, `fetch` hook, `config.schema`, `config.defaults`, `models[]`) plus standalone CLI.
- Thinking-cache: LRU(100) thinking-signature preservation across turns (without which Claude 4-6 returns 400).
- Google search: `googleSearch` plus `urlContext` tool injection, grounding-chunk citation extraction, markdown rendering.
- Image generation: `gemini-3.1-flash-image` model, BLOCK_NONE safety settings, configurable aspect ratio, base64 image persistence.
- 9router and OmniRoute: logical to physical model mapping, PID-offset anti-fingerprint (0 to 1000 ms `user_prompt_id` randomization).
- Version-dynamic: two-tier resolver (auto-updater plus npm plus GitHub plus `storage.googleapis.com/antigravity-version/version.json`, governed fallback 1.19.2, blocked list 1.11.5, 1.12.0, 1.12.3, 1.13.2, min supported 1.15.8).
- Tool-hardening: tool-name regex `/^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,63}$/`, dedupe, max 128 tools per request, Claude `[hardening]` description prefix.
- Schema-cleaner: recursive JSON-schema sanitizer (10 allowed keys, `additionalProperties` and `default` rewritten into descriptions, type uppercased for Antigravity strict parser).
- Runtime constraint: only `node:*` builtins plus global `fetch`. Zero npm runtime dependencies. Peers `@opencode-ai/sdk` and `@opencode-ai/plugin` are optional.
